hof_ic_role_remove_nikolay_bukharin = {
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_idea_nikolay_bukharin } remove_ideas = hof_ic_idea_nikolay_bukharin }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_idea_nikolay_bukharin_2 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_idea_nikolay_bukharin_2 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_idea_nikolay_bukharin_3 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_idea_nikolay_bukharin_3 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_0 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_0 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_1 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_1 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_3 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_3 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_7 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_7 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_f } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_f }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_1f } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_1f }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_3f } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_3f }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_7f } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_7f }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_80 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_80 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_17f } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_17f }
}

hof_ic_role_refresh_nikolay_bukharin = {
	hof_ic_role_remove_nikolay_bukharin = yes
	if = {
		limit = { hof_ic_seated_nikolay_bukharin = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_bukharin_economics_of_transition }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_bukharin_nep_is_not_a_retreat }

			}
			add_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_bukharin_0
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_bukharin_economics_of_transition

				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_bukharin_socialism_in_one_country }
			}

hof_ic_role_remove_lev_kamenev = {
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_idea_lev_kamenev } remove_ideas = hof_ic_idea_lev_kamenev }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_idea_lev_kamenev_2 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_idea_lev_kamenev_2 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_idea_lev_kamenev_3 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_idea_lev_kamenev_3 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_lev_kamenev_0 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_lev_kamenev_0 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_lev_kamenev_1 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_lev_kamenev_1 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_lev_kamenev_3 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_lev_kamenev_3 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_lev_kamenev_7 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_lev_kamenev_7 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_lev_kamenev_f } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_lev_kamenev_f }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_lev_kamenev_1f } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_lev_kamenev_1f }
}

hof_ic_role_remove_ivar_smilga = {
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_idea_ivar_smilga } remove_ideas = hof_ic_idea_ivar_smilga }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_idea_ivar_smilga_2 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_idea_ivar_smilga_2 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_idea_ivar_smilga_3 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_idea_ivar_smilga_3 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_ivar_smilga_0 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_ivar_smilga_0 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_ivar_smilga_1 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_ivar_smilga_1 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_ivar_smilga_3 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_ivar_smilga_3 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_ivar_smilga_7 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_ivar_smilga_7 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_ivar_smilga_f } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_ivar_smilga_f }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_ivar_smilga_1f } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_ivar_smilga_1f }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_ivar_smilga_3f } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_ivar_smilga_3f }
}

	hof_ic_role_remove_ivar_smilga = yes
	if = {
		limit = { hof_ic_seated_ivar_smilga = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_smilga_finland }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_smilga_fuel_main_directorate }

	hof_ic_role_remove_aleksandr_shlyapnikov = yes
	if = {
		limit = { hof_ic_seated_aleksandr_shlyapnikov = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {

	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_idea_nikolay_voznesensky_2 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_idea_nikolay_voznesensky_2 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_idea_nikolay_voznesensky_3 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_idea_nikolay_voznesensky_3 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_idea_nikolay_voznesensky_4 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_idea_nikolay_voznesensky_4 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_0 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_0 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_1 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_1 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_3 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_3 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_7 } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_7 }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_f } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_f }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_1f } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_1f }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_3f } remove_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_3f }
}

hof_ic_role_refresh_nikolay_voznesensky = {
	hof_ic_role_remove_nikolay_voznesensky = yes
	if = {
		limit = { hof_ic_seated_nikolay_voznesensky = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_fight_for_the_plan }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_leningrad_plan }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_state_planning_committee }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_placement_of_plants }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_ammunition_plan }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_war_economy }
			}
			add_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_0
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_fight_for_the_plan
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_leningrad_plan }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_state_planning_committee }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_placement_of_plants }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_ammunition_plan }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_war_economy }
			}
			add_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_1
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_fight_for_the_plan
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_leningrad_plan
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_state_planning_committee }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_placement_of_plants }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_ammunition_plan }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_war_economy }
			}
			add_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_3
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_fight_for_the_plan
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_leningrad_plan
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_state_planning_committee
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_placement_of_plants }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_ammunition_plan }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_war_economy }
			}
			add_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_7
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_fight_for_the_plan
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_leningrad_plan
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_state_planning_committee
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_placement_of_plants
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_ammunition_plan }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_war_economy }
			}
			add_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_f
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_fight_for_the_plan
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_leningrad_plan
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_state_planning_committee
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_placement_of_plants
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_ammunition_plan
				NOT = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_war_economy }
			}
			add_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_1f
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_fight_for_the_plan
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_leningrad_plan
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_state_planning_committee
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_placement_of_plants
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_ammunition_plan
				has_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_war_economy
			}
			add_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_3f
		}
		else = {
			add_ideas = hof_ic_role_nikolay_voznesensky_0
		}
	}
}
